## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- text-generation-inference is a Kubernetes component.
- deploying 1 workload(s) (inference-server) with 1 container(s).
- exposing 1 service(s) on ports\: 8033.
- connecting to external services\: grpc.
- Python k8s client with 25 API call(s).
